None)), ÁÉÍÓÚÜÑáéíóúüñ, AEIOUUNAEIOUUN), [^A-Z0-9], , 1) = TEL) THEN telefono WHEN (regexp_replace(translate(upper(trim(canal#49, None)), ÁÉÍÓÚÜÑáéíóúüñ, AEIOUUNAEIOUUN), [^A-Z0-9], , 1) = TELFONO) THEN telefono ELSE otro_canal END AS canal_silver#856, cast(cantidad#41 as double) AS cantidad_num#1059, cast(precio_unit#42 as double) AS precio_num#1080]

(6) Project
